**345. [src/i18n/locales/de.json:2008] Troubleshooting wizard points users to the 'Empty Feed Fixer-Tool' by its English name, but the tool is titled 'Leeren Feed Beheben' in the German UI.**

- Evidence: troubleshootingWizard.solutions.newUserFeed.steps[0]: "Gehe zum Empty Feed Fixer-Tool unten" while emptyFeedFixer.title is "Leeren Feed Beheben" — a German user following the instruction will not find a tool with that name on the page.
- Fix: Change the step to "Gehe unten zum Tool 'Leeren Feed Beheben'" (matching emptyFeedFixer.title), or rename consistently in both places.
**346. [src/i18n/locales/de.json:2581] Eight untranslated English strings remain in the client comparison table pros/cons.**

- Evidence: clientComparisonTable.clients.coracle.cons[0]: "Desktop only" (line 2581); current.pros[3]: "Clean UI" (2593); current.cons[0]: "More complex" (2596); habla.pros[3]: "Monetization" (2608); nostur.pros[0]: "Great UI" (2620); nostur.pros[1]: "Fast" (2621); nostur.pros[3]: "Active dev" (2623); plebstr.pros[1]: "Clean design" (2635). Sibling entries are translated (e.g. damus.cons[0] "Nur iOS").
- Fix: Translate: "Desktop only" → "Nur Desktop"; "Clean UI" → "Aufgeräumte Oberfläche"; "More complex" → "Komplexer"; "Monetization" → "Monetarisierung"; "Great UI" → "Tolle Oberfläche"; "Fast" → "Schnell"; "Active dev" → "Aktive Entwicklung"; "Clean design" → "Aufgeräumtes Design".
**347. [src/i18n/locales/en.json:810] Wallet of Satoshi is used as the canonical custodial-wallet example, but it moved to self-custody in 2025.**

- Evidence: guides.zapsAndLightning.quiz.questions[4].explanation (id custodial-vs-self): "Custodial wallets (like Wallet of Satoshi) are user-friendly but the provider controls your funds." (en.json line 810; also "custodial like Wallet of Satoshi" at line 766). Verified via 2025 coverage (Bitcoin Magazine, walletofsatoshi.com/disclosure): WoS integrated Spark and launched a self-custodial wallet, discontinued issuing Lightning invoices on the legacy custodial wallet in US/EU/AU/NZ with remaining regions to follow, and set a 2027 balance-migration deadline. The example now teaches the wrong category.
- Fix: Swap the custodial example to one that is custodial in 2026 (e.g. Primal wallet or Coinos): "Custodial wallets (like Primal's built-in wallet) are user-friendly but the provider controls your funds. Self-custodial wallets (like Zeus) give you full control but more responsibility." Keep Phoenix only if a second self-custodial example is wanted.
**348. [src/i18n/locales/en.json:2160] Troubleshooting wizard's no-wallet fix names wallets that cannot complete its own NWC step.**

- Evidence: troubleshootingWizard.solutions.noWallet.steps: "Download a lightning wallet (Alby, Zeus, Phoenix)" (en.json line 2160) followed by "Connect wallet to your Nostr client via NWC" (line 2162). Verified: Phoenix mobile has no NWC support (open issue ACINQ/phoenix#537; only the separate phoenixd server offers it), and "Alby" is no longer a simple downloadable wallet — the extension requires Alby Hub (self-hosted or paid cloud). Of the three, only Zeus (NWC since v0.12) satisfies the NWC step. A beginner picking Phoenix gets stuck at step 3.
- Fix: Change the wallet list to ones with built-in NWC that a creator can just install: "Download a lightning wallet that supports Nostr Wallet Connect (Zeus, Coinos, or Alby Hub + Alby Go)". Keep the remaining steps as-is; they then work for every listed option.
**349. [src/i18n/locales/en.json:2572] Coracle is described as a desktop-only client; it is a web client that runs in any browser.**

- Evidence: clientComparisonTable.clients.coracle.description: "A desktop client for power users with advanced features." with cons "Desktop only" and "Not mobile friendly" (en.json lines 2572-2585); ClientComparisonTable.tsx maps it as coracle: { desktop: 'https://coracle.social' }. coracle.social is a web app (verified live, HTTP 200) — there is no desktop download; it opens fine in mobile browsers too. Users filtering the table by 'Web' will never see it, and 'Desktop' filterers expect an installable app.
- Fix: Change the platform mapping to web ({ web: 'https://coracle.social' }), reword the description to "A web client for power users with advanced relay and community features", and replace the "Desktop only" con with something accurate (e.g. "No native mobile app").
**350. [src/i18n/locales/en.json:788] Zap privacy explanation claims Lightning transactions are publicly visible; the real exposure is Nostr zap receipts.**

- Evidence: guides.zapsAndLightning.quiz.questions[3] (id zap-privacy): correct option "The amount and sender are visible on Lightning..." (en.json line 780) and explanation "Lightning payments are pseudonymous like Bitcoin. The transaction is visible but not tied to your identity..." (line 788). This is the wrong mechanism: Lightning has no public ledger — payments are not publicly visible like on-chain Bitcoin. What makes zap sender and amount public is the NIP-57 zap receipt (kind 9735, containing the signed zap request with the sender's pubkey and amount) published to relays. The current text gives creators a false mental model of where the leak is.
- Fix: Reword option b to "The sender and amount are published on Nostr as a public zap receipt, but you can use different wallets/identities to separate them" and the explanation to: "Zaps create a public receipt on Nostr showing who zapped whom and how much (unless you zap anonymously). The Lightning payment itself isn't on a public ledger — the receipt is what everyone sees. Use separate wallets and identities if that linkage matters."
**351. [src/i18n/locales/en.json:212] Security quiz marks a genuinely reasonable practice (throwaway key for a new app) as a wrong answer without explaining why.**

- Evidence: guides.keysAndSecurity.quiz.questions[2] (id suspicious-client): option "Generate a throwaway key and migrate later" (en.json line 212) is a distractor, but using a throwaway key to test an untrusted client is legitimately good security practice; the flawed part ("migrate later" — Nostr has no key migration, followers don't transfer) is never addressed in the explanation (line 216), which only restates the signer answer. Audit criterion: distractors must be clearly wrong.
- Fix: Either (a) keep the option but extend the explanation: "A throwaway key is fine for a quick look, but there's no real 'migrate later' on Nostr — followers and reputation don't transfer between keys, so a signer is the sustainable answer"; or (b) replace the distractor with something unambiguously wrong (e.g. "Paste your nsec but delete the app afterwards").
**352. [src/i18n/locales/en.json:2377] Empty Feed Fixer recommends Purple Pages, a metadata-only relay that cannot put any posts in a feed.**

- Evidence: emptyFeedFixer.relays.wssPurplePages.description: "Metadata relay" (en.json line 2377), surfaced in step 2 whose promise is "Connect to relays to see content" (emptyFeedFixer.step2.description) with the success message "...connected to {relays} relays. Your Nostr feed should now have content!". purplepag.es (EmptyFeedFixer.tsx:174) only accepts profile/relay-list events (kinds 0, 3, 10002) — it serves no notes, so it does nothing for an empty feed, and a beginner cannot know that "Metadata relay" means "no posts here".
- Fix: Either swap purplepag.es for a general-purpose content relay in EmptyFeedFixer.tsx, or keep it and change the description key to plain language that manages expectations: "Profiles only — helps names and avatars load (no posts)".
**353. [src/i18n/locales/es.json:1282] Privacy quiz's marked-correct answer "Todas las anteriores" (all of the above) sits in the middle of the option list, so it excludes the option shown after it**

- Evidence: guides.privacySecurity.quiz.questions[3]: prompt "¿Qué metadatos pueden traicionarte?", options in render order: a "Ubicación GPS", b "Todas las anteriores" (correctId), c "EXIF en fotos". As displayed, "all of the previous" only covers option a, so the marked answer is not clearly right and the question is unanswerable as written.
- Fix: If keeping this question, reorder so the catch-all is last (a: Ubicación GPS, b: EXIF en fotos, c: "Todas las anteriores" with correctId c) and use "delatarte" instead of "traicionarte" ("¿Qué metadatos pueden delatarte?"). Preferably replaced wholesale by the current EN 'correlation-attacks' question when the quiz set is re-translated.
**354. [src/i18n/locales/es.json:1691] outboxModel quiz explanation left verbatim in English**

- Evidence: guides.outboxModel.quiz.questions[4].explanation = "When you update your relay list (kind:10002), followers automatically start checking your new relays. No manual reconnection needed!" — identical to en.json while the question and options around it are translated.
- Fix: Translate: "Cuando actualizas tu lista de relays (kind:10002), tus seguidores empiezan a consultar automáticamente los nuevos relays. ¡No hace falta reconectar nada manualmente!"
**355. [src/i18n/locales/es.json:885] Troubleshooting quiz marks "use your backup seed phrase" as a wrong answer to losing your private key, though restoring from backup is exactly right when one exists**

- Evidence: guides.troubleshooting.quiz.questions[2]: prompt "¿Qué hacer si pierdes tu llave privada?", correctId b "Crear cuenta nueva", with option c "Usar seed phrase de respaldo" marked wrong. The marked answer only holds when no backup exists (the explanation admits it: "Si no tienes backup, debes crear cuenta nueva"), so a user who answers "restore from backup" is told the safe behaviour is wrong.
- Fix: Either constrain the prompt ("¿Qué hacer si pierdes tu llave privada y no tienes ninguna copia de seguridad?") or replace with the current EN 'connection-issues' question when the troubleshooting quiz is re-translated from en.json.
**356. [src/i18n/locales/es.json:1947] troubleshootingWizard uses "relay" as an invariant plural in 19+ strings, ungrammatical in Spanish and inconsistent with "relays" used everywhere else in the file**

- Evidence: troubleshootingWizard.questions.relayCheck.options.offline = "Los relay aparecen como offline" (line 1947); also "Conecta a 3-5 relay recomendados" (2010), "Agrega más relay (apunta a 5-10 relay diversos)" (2025), "Todos los relay offline" (2064), "Mantén solo 3-5 relay estables" (2117) and 14 more in the same section, plus nip05Checker.results.valid.recommendedRelays = "Relay recomendados" (2278) and clientComparisonTable.clients.nostur.pros[2] = "Buena gestión de relay" (2555). Meanwhile emptyFeedFixer, relayExplorer, relayPlayground and quickstartSimulator consistently use "relays" ("Conectar a Todos los Relays Recomendados").
- Fix: Pluralize to "relays" throughout troubleshootingWizard, nip05Checker.results.valid.recommendedRelays ("Relays recomendados") and nostur.pros[2] ("Buena gestión de relays"), matching the convention already used in the rest of es.json.
**357. [src/i18n/locales/hi.json:986] Garbled text "इमेज शो हिंग" (broken transliteration of "image showing") in a quiz explanation**

- Evidence: guides.multiClient.quiz.questions[0].explanation (id "why-multiple", line 986): "विभिन्न Nostr क्लाइंट अलग-अलग विशेषताएं प्रदान करते हैं - कुछ बेहतर इमेज शो हिंग, कुछ बेहतर ज़ैप इंटीग्रेशन, कुछ बेहतर लंबी फॉर्म कंटेंट।" - "इमेज शो हिंग" is a corrupted word split and "लंबी फॉर्म कंटेंट" is an unassimilated calque.
- Fix: Replace the middle clause with natural Hindi: "विभिन्न Nostr क्लाइंट अलग-अलग विशेषताएं देते हैं - कुछ में इमेज बेहतर दिखती हैं, कुछ में ज़ैप इंटीग्रेशन बेहतर है, और कुछ लंबे लेखों के लिए बेहतर हैं। आपकी पहचान सभी में एक समान रहती है।"
**358. [src/i18n/locales/hi.json:1587] Outbox-model quiz prompt says the model solves a problem "in traditional social media", contradicting its own correct answer**

- Evidence: guides.outboxModel.quiz.questions[0].prompt (id "outbox-problem", line 1587): "पारंपरिक सोशल मीडिया में आउटबॉक्स मॉडल किस समस्या को हल करता है?" - the outbox model addresses discoverability in DECENTRALIZED networks; the marked correct answer (options[1], "विकेन्द्रीकृत नेटवर्क में दृश्यता की कमी") says exactly that, so the prompt's premise contradicts the answer and confuses the reader (the marked answer is still the only sensible pick, so it is not answer-breaking).
- Fix: Change the prompt to: "आउटबॉक्स मॉडल विकेन्द्रीकृत सोशल मीडिया की किस समस्या को हल करता है?" (replace पारंपरिक with विकेन्द्रीकृत and rephrase).
**359. [src/i18n/locales/hi.json:1823] Hindi keyGenerator description drops the security reassurance that keys are generated locally and never sent to a server**

- Evidence: keyGenerator.description (line 1823): "अपनी Nostr कुंजियाँ उत्पन्न करें - सार्वजनिक (npub) जो आपकी पहचान है और निजी (nsec) जो आपके खाते को नियंत्रित करती है।" - en (and pl/es/de, verified) all say: "Create a secure key pair to access Nostr. Your keys are generated locally in your browser and never sent to any server." The local-generation guarantee is security-relevant for a tool that produces a real nsec; hi is the only audited locale missing it.
- Fix: Replace with a translation of the current English: "Nostr के लिए एक सुरक्षित कुंजी जोड़ी बनाएं। आपकी कुंजियाँ आपके ब्राउज़र में ही स्थानीय रूप से उत्पन्न होती हैं और कभी किसी सर्वर को नहीं भेजी जातीं।"
**360. [src/i18n/locales/hi.json:2320] Hindi emptyFeedFixer description describes a diagnostic tool, but the component is a follow-accounts/connect-relays onboarding wizard**

- Evidence: emptyFeedFixer.description (line 2320): "तुरंत Nostr फीड को आबाद करें। समस्या का पता लगाएं और समाधान प्राप्त करें।" ("diagnose the issue and get solutions") - en reads: "New to Nostr? Start by following some accounts and connecting to relays to see content in your feed." The hi step1/step2/step3 keys already match the current follow/connect flow, so this intro promises a diagnosis the tool does not perform.
- Fix: Replace with: "Nostr पर नए हैं? फीड में सामग्री देखने के लिए कुछ खातों को फॉलो करके और रिले से कनेक्ट होकर शुरुआत करें।"
**361. [src/i18n/locales/hi.json:2720] Hindi relayPlayground description says "experiment with relay commands in a safe environment" though the tool connects to real relays in real time**

- Evidence: relayPlayground.description (line 2720): "एक सुरक्षित वातावरण में Nostr रिले कमांड के साथ प्रयोग करें" - en reads: "Test and explore Nostr relays in real-time. Check connectivity, inspect relay info, and analyze events." The tool's own tabs (connection, health, NIPs, live events, query) do live relay work; "safe environment" wrongly implies a sandbox and "relay commands" describes nothing in the UI.
- Fix: Replace with: "रीयल-टाइम में Nostr रिले जांचें और एक्सप्लोर करें। कनेक्टिविटी जांचें, रिले की जानकारी देखें, और इवेंट्स का विश्लेषण करें।"
**362. [src/i18n/locales/hi.json:439] Guide-card description "Nostr रिले डीप में समझें" is a broken half-translation of "deep dive"**

- Evidence: guides.relaysDemystified.description (line 439): "Nostr रिले डीप में समझें - वे कैसे काम करते हैं, उन्हें कैसे चुनें, और उनके साथ कैसे काम करें।" - "डीप में समझें" is not grammatical Hindi; this string is user-visible on the guides index card and in meta descriptions.
- Fix: Change to: "Nostr रिले को गहराई से समझें - वे कैसे काम करते हैं, उन्हें कैसे चुनें, और उनका उपयोग कैसे करें।"
**363. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:1691] One quiz explanation in the outbox model quiz is left entirely in English.**

- Evidence: guides.outboxModel.quiz.questions[4].explanation (id "switching-relays") = "When you update your relay list (kind:10002), followers automatically start checking your new relays. No manual reconnection needed!" — the only untranslated string in the PL extract; question and options around it are Polish.
- Fix: Replace with: "Gdy zaktualizujesz swoją listę relayów (kind:10002), obserwujący automatycznie zaczną sprawdzać Twoje nowe relaye. Nie trzeba niczego łączyć ręcznie!"
**364. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:498] PL quizzes teach "5-15 relayów" as the correct count in two guides while the outbox model quiz marks "10-15 relay" wrong and "2-4 relay" correct.**

- Evidence: guides.relaysDemystified.quiz.questions[2] (id "how-many") correct label "5-15 relayów" (line 498) and guides.relayGuide.quiz.questions[1] (id "how-many") correct label "5-15" (line 1119, explanation line 1127 "5-15 relayów to złoty środek"), versus guides.outboxModel.quiz.questions[3] (id "optimal-relay-count") where "2-4 relay" (line 1657) is correct and "10-15 relay" (line 1661) is explicitly wrong. EN uses 3-10 in the corresponding spots, which does not overlap the outbox quiz's wrong answer.
- Fix: Change both PL "5-15" answers and their explanations to "3-10" (matching EN relaysDemystified relay-count and troubleshooting slow-performance), leaving the outbox quiz's 2-4 as the outbox-specific best practice.
**365. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:1282] "Wszystkie powyższe" is the middle option, so the correct answer literally excludes the option listed below it.**

- Evidence: guides.privacySecurity.quiz.questions[3] (id "metadata-leaks") options in render order: a "Lokalizacja GPS", b "Wszystkie powyższe" (correctId), c "EXIF w zdjęciach". PrivacySecurityQuiz.tsx maps options in JSON order without shuffling, so "all of the above" appears above EXIF and logically covers only GPS.
- Fix: Reorder to a "Lokalizacja GPS", b "EXIF w zdjęciach", c "Wszystkie powyższe" and set correctId to "c" (or rephrase b to "Wszystkie wymienione w tym quizie").
**366. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:996] The multi-client sync question's correct answer claims settings sync between clients, which is false for app settings and contradicted by its own explanation.**

- Evidence: guides.multiClient.quiz.questions[1] (id "what-syncs") correct label: "Wszystko - posty, kontakty, ustawienia". Client app settings do not sync (only data stored as relay events does); the explanation itself says "posty, kontakty, metadane", not "ustawienia". A user switching clients will find their settings gone.
- Fix: Change the label to "Posty, kontakty i profil - wszystko, co jest zapisane na relayach" and keep the explanation as is.
**367. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:10] 12 of 13 PL quizzes are independently written question sets, not translations of the EN source, which breaks the EN-as-source maintenance model and has already produced internal contradictions.**

- Evidence: EN guides.whatIsNostr.quiz.questions[0] is id "nostr-acronym" ("What does NOSTR stand for?"); PL guides.whatIsNostr.quiz.questions[0] is id "protocol-vs-platform" ("Czym jest Nostr?"). Verified across both full files: only guides.outboxModel.quiz is a faithful translation; elsewhere only isolated questions match (relay-ban, community-building, sharing-keys id). The divergence produced the contradictions reported above (Lightning address, relay counts, suspicious-client).
- Fix: Decide policy explicitly: either regenerate PL quizzes from EN via the uzupelnij-tlumaczenia flow (keeping the good PL-only questions where EN has no counterpart), or document PL quizzes as an intentionally independent set so future audits and EN content updates do not silently strand PL.
**368. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:438] Guide title "Relaye Rozwiane" is a broken calque of "Relays Demystified" — in Polish "rozwiane" works only for doubts, so the title reads as "relays blown away".**

- Evidence: guides.relaysDemystified.title = "Relaye Rozwiane" (EN: "Relays Demystified"). "Rozwiać" collocates with "wątpliwości"; applied to relays it means dispersed/wind-blown. Prominent guide title shown in navigation and page header.
- Fix: Rename to "Relaye bez tajemnic".
**369. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:582] The PL NIP-05 quiz dropped the only security question of the EN set — the warning that NIP-05 can deanonymize you.**

- Evidence: EN guides.nip05Identity.quiz has question id "nip05-security" ("It links your identity to a domain you control, which could deanonymize you", severity warning). The PL quiz (guides.nip05Identity.quiz, questions: purpose, benefits, required, setup-location, centralization) contains no privacy/deanonymization content at all.
- Fix: Add a translated question mirroring nip05-security, e.g. prompt "Co warto wiedzieć o NIP-05 i prywatności?", correct: "Łączy Twoją tożsamość z domeną, co może ujawnić, kim jesteś", explanation per EN.
**370. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:818] PL guide descriptions are systematically truncated to stubs versus the EN source, losing concrete information (and meta-description value if used for SEO).**

(repetitive). Pattern affects most guides.* entries.
- Fix: Retranslate the full EN descriptions for all guides.*.description keys; for privacySecurity use e.g. "Zaawansowane praktyki prywatności i bezpieczeństwa dla wymagających użytkowników."
**371. [src/i18n/locales/pl.json:1960] Three troubleshooting-wizard strings are ungrammatical Polish around the word "zap".**

(line 2092). All are visible UI text.

**372. [src/i18n/locales/zh.json:1312] The signer-extension explanation says keys are stored "in an external client" instead of "outside the client", muddling the one security fact the question exists to teach.**

The correct option's label at line 1300 states it properly ("密钥保存在扩展中，不会暴露给客户端"), so the explanation contradicts the answer it explains.

### low
**373. [src/content/guides/ar/troubleshooting.mdx:33] LTR arrows (→) used throughout Arabic RTL prose, pointing against the reading direction and inconsistent with sibling guides that use ←.**

- Evidence: "تحقق من الإعدادات → Relays، تأكد من الاتصال بـ 3-5 relays" (line 33); also lines 178, 202, 203, 367, 384-388 (12 occurrences). Sibling ar guides use the RTL-correct arrow: ar/relay-guide.mdx line 296 "تبويب الملف الشخصي ← الإعدادات" and ar/relays-demystified.mdx line 199 "الإعدادات ← Relays ← إضافة".
- Fix: Replace every → with ← in this file's prose (all 12 occurrences are prose, none in code blocks), including English-labeled paths like "App Store → Updates", matching the convention already used in ar/relay-guide.mdx ("الإعدادات ← Network").
**374. [src/content/guides/ar/zaps-and-lightning.mdx:22] LTR arrows (→) in Arabic RTL prose in the How-Zaps-Work steps and setup instructions, inconsistent with ← used in the other Arabic relay guides.**

- Evidence: "**أنت تنشئ محتوى جيدًا** → منشور أو سلسلة أو رد" (line 22, likewise lines 23-26), and "تحرير الملف الشخصي → ابحث عن حقل \"Lightning Address\"... → الصق → احفظ" (line 224); also lines 185 and 432. Contrast ar/relays-demystified.mdx line 199 which uses ←.
- Fix: Replace → with ← in all prose occurrences (lines 22-26, 185, 224, 432). Leave the fenced code block at lines 289-292 ("Client → Profile → Edit → Lightning Address") unchanged, since code blocks stay in English.
**375. [src/content/guides/de/keys-and-security.mdx:166] "1 externes Lager" mistranslates "1 offsite location" in the 3-2-1 backup rule ("Lager" = warehouse).**

- Evidence: Line 166: "**1 externes Lager**" for EN "**1 offsite location**" — a German reader parses "Lager" as a storage depot, muddying the third pillar of the security rule.
- Fix: Replace with "**1 Kopie an einem anderen Ort**"; adjust the next bullet if needed ("Cloud-Speicher zählt als anderer Ort (wenn verschlüsselt)").
**376. [src/content/guides/de/outbox-model.mdx:7] Frontmatter drifts from EN source: category downgraded to "beginner" and estimatedTime changed**

- Evidence: Line 5: estimatedTime: "15 min" and line 7: category: "beginner" — EN source (src/content/guides/en/outbox-model.mdx lines 5-7) has estimatedTime: "10-15 minutes" and category: "intermediate". German readers see the guide labeled with a different difficulty than every other locale.
- Fix: In the DE frontmatter set category: "intermediate" and estimatedTime: "10-15 Minuten" (matching the "Minuten" style used by the other DE guides).
**377. [src/content/guides/de/zaps-and-lightning.mdx:325] "Collaborators" is rendered as "Mitarbeiter" (employees), shifting the meaning for a creator audience.**

- Evidence: Line 325: "teile die Zahlung automatisch mit Mitarbeitern" and line 340: "**Mitarbeiter hinzufügen**: Gib ihre Lightning Addresses ein" (EN: "split the payment with collaborators" / "Add collaborators"). "Mitarbeiter" implies an employment relationship; podcast guests and co-authors are not that.
- Fix: Use "Mitwirkende": "teile die Zahlung automatisch mit allen Mitwirkenden" and "**Mitwirkende hinzufügen**". (Avoid "Kollaborateure", which carries a negative historical connotation in German.)
**378. [src/content/guides/en/faq.mdx:148] The guide is nearly 100% label-bullet fragments, and eight later answers duplicate their opening paragraph verbatim as a bullet list immediately below it.**

- Evidence: Duplication pattern, e.g. 'Why are some of my messages not delivering?' (lines 544-559): the lead paragraph says "recipient is offline, relays are down, or you're not connected to the same relays... Use multiple relays", then 'Common causes'/'Solutions' bullets restate every clause. Same paragraph-then-restating-bullets shape in 'How do I verify someone's identity?' (563-573), 'What happens if a relay deletes my content?' (586-593), 'Can I edit or delete posts?' (606-613), 'Why do I see different content on different clients?' (626-634), 'How do I backup my entire Nostr history?' (648-655), 'How do I run my own relay?' (696-703), 'Are my DMs actually private?' (726-739). Pure bullet-fragment sections with no prose at all: 'Which Nostr client should I use?' (148-177), 'How do I find people to follow?' (407-439), 'What are zaps?' (447-488), 'Why is my feed empty?' (328-358).
- Fix: For the eight duplicated answers, keep either the paragraph or the bullets, not both. For the four all-fragment answers, open each with 2-3 sentences of actual prose that answer the question before any list. FAQ format tolerates bullets, so this is a trim-and-top pass, not a full rewrite.

**380. [src/content/guides/en/keys-and-security.mdx:89] The guide is almost entirely bullet fragments; several conceptual sections need rewriting into flowing prose.**

(lines 89-97, three bold-label bullets), 'The Rule Explained' (lines 153-170, fragment bullets like "One isn't enough. Two is better. Three is safe."), the rationale lines under each DON'T/DO in 'Security Best Practices' (lines 205-245), and 'What you do with your npub' (lines 56-61).

**381. [src/content/guides/en/nip17-private-messages.mdx:148] Roughly the back half of the guide is still bullet fragments; six sections need rewriting into prose**

The rest is fragment-style: "Migration Guide" Steps 1-5 (150-232), "Client-Specific Setup Guides" (234-263), "Security Best Practices" (264-303, six numbered mini-headings over one-line bullets), Troubleshooting items 2-6 (327-353, bare Cause/Solution pairs), "The Future of Private Messaging" (355-366), and the Conclusion checkmark list (374-380).

**382. [src/content/guides/en/nostr-tools.mdx:14] The guide is essentially 100% bullet fragments; the only flowing prose is the single intro sentence.**

- Evidence: Line 14 ("Nostr has a rich ecosystem of tools...") is the sole prose sentence; everything after is "**URL:** / **What it does:** / **Best for:**" bullet blocks. Directory format excuses per-tool bullets, but no category has even a one-sentence intro telling a creator why they need the category (e.g. that images are not stored on relays, which is why media hosting exists; why a NIP-05 name matters before someone impersonates you).
- Fix: Add a 2-3 sentence prose intro to each major section explaining why a creator needs this category, prioritizing: Media Hosting, Identity & Verification (NIP-05), Lightning & Zaps, Privacy & Security (signers), Key Management, Analytics & Discovery, Relay Tools. Keep the per-tool bullet cards as they are.

**386. [src/content/guides/en/troubleshooting.mdx:15] The guide is effectively 100% label-bullet fragments with a single prose sentence (misrendered as a heading); every Problem section jumps into Symptoms bullets with zero connective explanation**

Not following anyone** - **Cause**: New account with no follows - **Solution**: ..."). The only flowing sentences in the body are line 11 (styled as an H2) and lines 241, 430.
